around an installation instead of actually building it, even though I know the deadline is slipping.\n- key: S033\n  name: Elevated_Irritable_Mood\n  description: A distinct period of abnormally and persistently elevated, expansive, or irritable mood.\n  manifestation: For stretches I feel lit up and almost unstoppable, like every idea is brilliant, but then a small comment from a collaborator manifestation: I’ll suddenly take on three proposals, reorganize the studio, sketch all night, email curators, and start cutting materials for pieces I haven’t even budgeted yet.\n- key: S035\n  name: like I’m about to change how everyone understands installation art and that normal limits like funding, time, or experience don’t really apply to me.\n- key: S036\n  name: Decreased_Sleep\n duration of the active and residual portions of the illness\n  manifestation: Looking back over this whole period, the energized, irritable, overproductive stretches have taken up more of the year I don't really know how else to explain it.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think that the secret meanings you mention have a special significance that other people don't understand?\n\nPatient: I... well. I'll be thinking about a piece, and then suddenly there are images, and titles, and... the wording for a grant application... colors... things people said to me. It's a lot.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these difficulties with explaining your ideas and the special meanings you see in things are causing you a lot of stress or problems in your daily life?\n\nPatient: Yes, I suppose so. I'm not really sure what you mean by that. I get convinced it's meant specifically for me. Like it's an instruction, or... a warning about my work. \n\nSometimes people try to point out a more ordinary explanation for it, but... it's very hard for me to just let it go. It just feels too specific.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages or instructions you see in things are specifically intended for you, and not for anyone else?\n\nPatient: Yes.
